// Any completed close back through the frozen level invalidates the break state.
// Candle/participation quality is a separate gate for promoting that failure
// into a directional reversal opportunity; it must not hide the invalidation.
bool bullishBreakFailed = bullishBreakFailureRaw
bool bearishBreakFailed = bearishBreakFailureRaw
bool qualifiedBullishBreakFailure = bullishBreakFailed and f_rejection_quality(false, sweepUpperWickShare, true, sweepVolumeAcceptance)
bool qualifiedBearishBreakFailure = bearishBreakFailed and f_rejection_quality(true, sweepLowerWickShare, true, sweepVolumeAcceptance)
bool bullishRetest = barstate.isconfirmed and insideRetestWindow and priorWatchedBreakDirection == 1 and not bullishBreakFailed and low <= priorWatchedBreakLevel + activeWatchedRetestTolerance and low >= priorWatchedBreakLevel - retestOvershoot and close > priorWatchedBreakLevel and closeLocation >= 0.55
bool bearishRetest = barstate.isconfirmed and insideRetestWindow and priorWatchedBreakDirection == -1 and not bearishBreakFailed and high >= priorWatchedBreakLevel - activeWatchedRetestTolerance and high <= priorWatchedBreakLevel + retestOvershoot and close < priorWatchedBreakLevel and closeLocation <= 0.45
float resolvedBreakEventLevel = bullishBreakFailed or bearishBreakFailed or bullishRetest or bearishRetest ? priorWatchedBreakLevel : na
bool fakeoutWindowExpired = priorWatchedBreakDirection != 0 and barsFromBreakAttempt > fakeoutWindow
bool retestWindowExpired = not priorWatchedBreakConfirmed or barsFromConfirmedBreak > retestWindow
bool breakWatcherExpired = priorWatchedBreakDirection != 0 and fakeoutWindowExpired and retestWindowExpired
bool breakWatcherResolved = bullishBreakFailed or bearishBreakFailed or bullishRetest or bearishRetest or breakWatcherExpired

if breakWatcherResolved
    watchedBreakDirection := 0
    watchedBreakLevel := na
    watchedBreakBar := na
    watchedBreakConfirmationBar := na
    watchedBreakBuffer := na
    watchedBreakRetestTolerance := na
    watchedBreakFailureBuffer := na
    watchedBreakConfirmed := false

// Arm on the first outside close. A later second close upgrades the same watch
// to confirmed; a return inside is classified as a failed break/liquidity grab.
bool bullishStructuralAttempt = strongResistanceBreakAttempt or higherResistanceBreakAttempt or historicalResistanceAttempt or bullishBreakAttempt
bool bearishStructuralAttempt = strongSupportBreakAttempt or higherSupportBreakAttempt or historicalSupportAttempt or bearishBreakAttempt
bool bullishStructuralBreak = strongResistanceBreak or higherResistanceBreak or historicalResistanceBreak or bullishBreak
bool bearishStructuralBreak = strongSupportBreak or higherSupportBreak or historicalSupportBreak or bearishBreak
float bullishAttemptLevel = strongResistanceBreakAttempt ? resistanceBreakCandidateLevel : higherResistanceBreakAttempt ? priorHigherTimeframeResistance : historicalResistanceAttempt ? priorHistoricalResistance : lastSwingHigh
float bearishAttemptLevel = strongSupportBreakAttempt ? supportBreakCandidateLevel : higherSupportBreakAttempt ? priorHigherTimeframeSupport : historicalSupportAttempt ? priorHistoricalSupport : lastSwingLow
float bullishConfirmedBreakLevel = strongResistanceBreak ? confirmedResistanceBreakLevel : higherResistanceBreak ? confirmedHigherResistance : historicalResistanceBreak ? confirmedHistoricalResistance : bullishBreak ? confirmedSwingHighBreak : na
float bearishConfirmedBreakLevel = strongSupportBreak ? confirmedSupportBreakLevel : higherSupportBreak ? confirmedHigherSupport : historicalSupportBreak ? confirmedHistoricalSupport : bearishBreak ? confirmedSwingLowBreak : na
if bullishStructuralBreak and not bearishStructuralBreak
    bool sameBullishWatch = watchedBreakDirection == 1 and not na(watchedBreakLevel) and not na(bullishConfirmedBreakLevel) and math.abs(watchedBreakLevel - bullishConfirmedBreakLevel) <= math.max(nz(watchedBreakBuffer, breakBuffer), syminfo.mintick * 2.0)
    watchedBreakDirection := 1
    watchedBreakLevel := nz(bullishConfirmedBreakLevel, bullishAttemptLevel)
    watchedBreakBar := sameBullishWatch and not na(watchedBreakBar) ? watchedBreakBar : bar_index
    watchedBreakConfirmationBar := bar_index
    watchedBreakBuffer := sameBullishWatch and not na(watchedBreakBuffer) ? watchedBreakBuffer : breakBuffer
    watchedBreakRetestTolerance := sameBullishWatch and not na(watchedBreakRetestTolerance) ? watchedBreakRetestTolerance : retestTolerance
    watchedBreakFailureBuffer := sameBullishWatch and not na(watchedBreakFailureBuffer) ? watchedBreakFailureBuffer : math.max(breakBuffer * 0.50, safeAtr * 0.04)
    watchedBreakConfirmed := true
else if bearishStructuralBreak and not bullishStructuralBreak
    bool sameBearishWatch = watchedBreakDirection == -1 and not na(watchedBreakLevel) and not na(bearishConfirmedBreakLevel) and math.abs(watchedBreakLevel - bearishConfirmedBreakLevel) <= math.max(nz(watchedBreakBuffer, breakBuffer), syminfo.mintick * 2.0)
    watchedBreakDirection := -1
    watchedBreakLevel := nz(bearishConfirmedBreakLevel, bearishAttemptLevel)
    watchedBreakBar := sameBearishWatch and not na(watchedBreakBar) ? watchedBreakBar : bar_index
    watchedBreakConfirmationBar := bar_index
    watchedBreakBuffer := sameBearishWatch and not na(watchedBreakBuffer) ? watchedBreakBuffer : breakBuffer
    watchedBreakRetestTolerance := sameBearishWatch and not na(watchedBreakRetestTolerance) ? watchedBreakRetestTolerance : retestTolerance
    watchedBreakFailureBuffer := sameBearishWatch and not na(watchedBreakFailureBuffer) ? watchedBreakFailureBuffer : math.max(breakBuffer * 0.50, safeAtr * 0.04)
    watchedBreakConfirmed := true
else if bullishStructuralAttempt and not bearishStructuralAttempt
    watchedBreakDirection := 1
    watchedBreakLevel := bullishAttemptLevel
    watchedBreakBar := bar_index
    watchedBreakConfirmationBar := na
    watchedBreakBuffer := breakBuffer
    watchedBreakRetestTolerance := retestTolerance
    watchedBreakFailureBuffer := math.max(breakBuffer * 0.50, safeAtr * 0.04)
    watchedBreakConfirmed := false
else if bearishStructuralAttempt and not bullishStructuralAttempt
    watchedBreakDirection := -1
    watchedBreakLevel := bearishAttemptLevel
    watchedBreakBar := bar_index
    watchedBreakConfirmationBar := na
    watchedBreakBuffer := breakBuffer
    watchedBreakRetestTolerance := retestTolerance
    watchedBreakFailureBuffer := math.max(breakBuffer * 0.50, safeAtr * 0.04)
    watchedBreakConfirmed := false

// Wyckoff spring/upthrust tests require a later revisit with a smaller result
// and without renewed high effort. The raw sweep alone remains a warning.
var int watchedSweepDirection = 0
var float watchedSweepLevel = na
var float watchedSweepExtreme = na
var int watchedSweepBar = na
if twoSidedLiquiditySweep
    watchedSweepDirection := 0
    watchedSweepLevel := na
    watchedSweepExtreme := na
    watchedSweepBar := na
else if bullishSweep
    watchedSweepDirection := 1
    watchedSweepLevel := lastSwingLow
    watchedSweepExtreme := low
    watchedSweepBar := bar_index
else if bearishSweep
    watchedSweepDirection := -1
    watchedSweepLevel := lastSwingHigh
    watchedSweepExtreme := high
    watchedSweepBar := bar_index

int barsFromSweep = na(watchedSweepBar) ? 100000 : bar_index - watchedSweepBar
bool insideSweepTestWindow = watchedSweepDirection != 0 and barsFromSweep > 0 and barsFromSweep <= retestWindow
bool quieterTestVolume = volumeAvailable and relativeVolume <= 1.0
bool quieterTestSpread = not na(relativeSpread) and relativeSpread <= 1.0
bool bullishWyckoffTest = barstate.isconfirmed and insideSweepTestWindow and watchedSweepDirection == 1 and low <= watchedSweepLevel + retestTolerance and low > watchedSweepExtreme and close > watchedSweepLevel and close > open and quieterTestVolume and quieterTestSpread
bool bearishWyckoffTest = barstate.isconfirmed and insideSweepTestWindow and watchedSweepDirection == -1 and high >= watchedSweepLevel - retestTolerance and high < watchedSweepExtreme and close < watchedSweepLevel and close < open and quieterTestVolume and quieterTestSpread
float wyckoffTestLevel = bullishWyckoffTest or bearishWyckoffTest ? watchedSweepLevel : na
if bullishWyckoffTest or bearishWyckoffTest or barsFromSweep > retestWindow
    watchedSweepDirection := 0
    watchedSweepLevel := na
    watchedSweepExtreme := na
    watchedSweepBar := na

// =============================================================================
// Wyckoff accumulation/distribution sequence
// =============================================================================
// by one entity. Phase A needs a prior trend plus effort; AR/ST build the range;
// Phase C is optional; Phase D needs a confirmed SOS/SOW and its retest.
var int wyckoffMode = 0              // 1 accumulation, -1 distribution, 0 none
var int wyckoffStage = 0             // event progression inside the active mode
var int wyckoffPhase = 0             // 1=A, 2=B, 3=C, 4=D, 5=E
var int wyckoffSequenceStartBar = na
var int wyckoffClimaxBar = na
var int wyckoffAutomaticBar = na
var int wyckoffSignBar = na
var int wyckoffPhaseEBar = na
var float wyckoffRangeSupport = na
var float wyckoffRangeResistance = na
var float wyckoffClimaxRvol = na
var float wyckoffClimaxSpread = na
var bool wyckoffResistanceWasBroken = false
var bool wyckoffSupportWasBroken = false
// Phase-C tests must remain tied to the exact Spring/UTAD that created them.
var int wyckoffPhaseCSweepDirection = 0
var float wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel = na
var float wyckoffPhaseCSweepExtreme = na
var int wyckoffPhaseCSweepBar = na

// Original accumulation must stop an extended downtrend; original distribution
// must stop an extended uptrend. Re-accumulation/re-distribution are intentionally
// not labelled by this state machine because their prior-trend rules are different.
int adaptiveWyckoffTrendMinimum = timeframe.isdaily ? 40 : timeframe.isweekly ? 26 : 12
int wyckoffPriorTrendBars = math.max(wyckoffTrendLookback, adaptiveWyckoffTrendMinimum)
int wyckoffTrendSlopeBars = math.max(3, int(math.round(float(wyckoffPriorTrendBars) * 0.25)))
float wyckoffMinimumTrendMoveAtr = chartSeconds < 24 * 60 * 60 ? 3.0 : 4.0
float priorWyckoffLow = ta.lowest(low[1], wyckoffPriorTrendBars)
float priorWyckoffHigh = ta.highest(high[1], wyckoffPriorTrendBars)
float wyckoffTrendStartClose = close[wyckoffPriorTrendBars + 1]
float wyckoffTrendReferenceAtr = nz(ta.sma(safeAtr[1], wyckoffPriorTrendBars), safeAtr)
float wyckoffLowerWick = math.min(open, close) - low
float wyckoffUpperWick = high - math.max(open, close)
bool wyckoffTrendHistoryReady = not na(wyckoffTrendStartClose) and not na(sma24[1]) and not na(sma50[1]) and not na(sma24[wyckoffTrendSlopeBars + 1]) and not na(sma50[wyckoffTrendSlopeBars + 1]) and not na(priorWyckoffLow) and not na(priorWyckoffHigh)
// Qualify the trend from bars preceding the stopping event. The climax candle
// may itself reverse sharply, so requiring its close to remain below/above the
// moving averages would contradict the purpose of SC/BC detection.
bool extendedDownwardMove = wyckoffTrendHistoryReady and wyckoffTrendStartClose - low >= wyckoffTrendReferenceAtr * wyckoffMinimumTrendMoveAtr
bool extendedUpwardMove = wyckoffTrendHistoryReady and high - wyckoffTrendStartClose >= wyckoffTrendReferenceAtr * wyckoffMinimumTrendMoveAtr
bool persistentDowntrend = wyckoffTrendHistoryReady and sma24[1] < sma50[1] and close[1] < sma24[1] and sma24[1] < sma24[wyckoffTrendSlopeBars + 1] and sma50[1] <= sma50[wyckoffTrendSlopeBars + 1]
bool persistentUptrend = wyckoffTrendHistoryReady and sma24[1] > sma50[1] and close[1] > sma24[1] and sma24[1] > sma24[wyckoffTrendSlopeBars + 1] and sma50[1] >= sma50[wyckoffTrendSlopeBars + 1]
bool priorDowntrendForWyckoff = extendedDownwardMove and persistentDowntrend
bool priorUptrendForWyckoff = extendedUpwardMove and persistentUptrend
bool preliminaryWyckoffEffort = volumeAvailable and (relativeVolume >= wyckoffPreliminaryVolume or statisticalVolumeHigh)
bool climaxWyckoffEffort = volumeAvailable and (relativeVolume >= wyckoffClimaxVolume or statisticalVolumeExtreme)
bool preliminarySupportRaw = barstate.isconfirmed and priorDowntrendForWyckoff and preliminaryWyckoffEffort and close < open and (vsaWideResult or wyckoffLowerWick >= candleRange * 0.15) and (na(priorWyckoffLow) or low <= priorWyckoffLow)
bool sellingClimaxRaw = barstate.isconfirmed and priorDowntrendForWyckoff and climaxWyckoffEffort and vsaWideResult and closeLocation >= 0.30 and wyckoffLowerWick >= candleRange * 0.15 and (na(priorWyckoffLow) or low <= priorWyckoffLow)
bool preliminarySupplyRaw = barstate.isconfirmed and priorUptrendForWyckoff and preliminaryWyckoffEffort and close > open and (vsaWideResult or wyckoffUpperWick >= candleRange * 0.15) and (na(priorWyckoffHigh) or high >= priorWyckoffHigh)
bool buyingClimaxRaw = barstate.isconfirmed and priorUptrendForWyckoff and climaxWyckoffEffort and vsaWideResult and closeLocation <= 0.70 and wyckoffUpperWick >= candleRange * 0.15 and (na(priorWyckoffHigh) or high >= priorWyckoffHigh)

bool wyckoffBuildingExpired = barstate.isconfirmed and wyckoffMode != 0 and wyckoffPhase < 5 and not na(wyckoffSequenceStartBar) and bar_index - wyckoffSequenceStartBar > wyckoffMaxSequenceBars
bool wyckoffSupportBreakRaw = f_confirmed_level_break(false, wyckoffRangeSupport, nz(wyckoffRangeSupport[1], wyckoffRangeSupport), breakBuffer, bearishExceptionalBreakBar)
bool wyckoffResistanceBreakRaw = f_confirmed_level_break(true, wyckoffRangeResistance, nz(wyckoffRangeResistance[1], wyckoffRangeResistance), breakBuffer, bullishExceptionalBreakBar)
bool wyckoffPhaseEOppositeBreak = barstate.isconfirmed and wyckoffPhase == 5 and ((wyckoffMode == 1 and (bearishChoch or not na(wyckoffRangeSupport) and wyckoffSupportBreakRaw)) or (wyckoffMode == -1 and (bullishChoch or not na(wyckoffRangeResistance) and wyckoffResistanceBreakRaw)))
bool wyckoffPhaseEExpired = barstate.isconfirmed and wyckoffMode != 0 and wyckoffPhase == 5 and not na(wyckoffPhaseEBar) and (bar_index - wyckoffPhaseEBar > contextLookback or wyckoffPhaseEOppositeBreak)
bool wyckoffSequenceExpired = wyckoffBuildingExpired or wyckoffPhaseEExpired
if wyckoffSequenceExpired
    wyckoffMode := 0
    wyckoffStage := 0
    wyckoffPhase := 0
    wyckoffSequenceStartBar := na
    wyckoffClimaxBar := na
    wyckoffAutomaticBar := na
    wyckoffSignBar := na
    wyckoffPhaseEBar := na
    wyckoffRangeSupport := na
    wyckoffRangeResistance := na
    wyckoffClimaxRvol := na
    wyckoffClimaxSpread := na
    wyckoffResistanceWasBroken := false
    wyckoffSupportWasBroken := false
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepDirection := 0
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel := na
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepExtreme := na
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepBar := na

bool preliminarySupportEvent = preliminarySupportRaw and not sellingClimaxRaw and (wyckoffMode == 0 or (wyckoffMode == 1 and wyckoffPhase == 5))
bool sellingClimaxEvent = sellingClimaxRaw and (wyckoffMode != 1 or wyckoffStage <= 1 or wyckoffPhase == 5)
bool preliminarySupplyEvent = preliminarySupplyRaw and not buyingClimaxRaw and not preliminarySupportEvent and not sellingClimaxEvent and (wyckoffMode == 0 or (wyckoffMode == -1 and wyckoffPhase == 5))
bool buyingClimaxEvent = buyingClimaxRaw and not sellingClimaxEvent and (wyckoffMode != -1 or wyckoffStage <= 1 or wyckoffPhase == 5)
bool startNewWyckoffModel = preliminarySupportEvent or preliminarySupplyEvent or (sellingClimaxEvent and (wyckoffMode != 1 or wyckoffPhase == 5)) or (buyingClimaxEvent and (wyckoffMode != -1 or wyckoffPhase == 5))

if startNewWyckoffModel
    // Fully clear the previous model before assigning the new event. Without
    // this reset, a fresh PS/PSY could inherit the old range boundary or climax
    // statistics and display a false completed range.
    wyckoffStage := 0
    wyckoffPhase := 0
    wyckoffSequenceStartBar := na
    wyckoffClimaxBar := na
    wyckoffAutomaticBar := na
    wyckoffSignBar := na
    wyckoffPhaseEBar := na
    wyckoffRangeSupport := na
    wyckoffRangeResistance := na
    wyckoffClimaxRvol := na
    wyckoffClimaxSpread := na
    wyckoffResistanceWasBroken := false
    wyckoffSupportWasBroken := false
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepDirection := 0
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel := na
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepExtreme := na
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepBar := na

int confirmedPivotBar = bar_index - pivotBars
float wyckoffRangeHeight = not na(wyckoffRangeSupport) and not na(wyckoffRangeResistance) ? math.max(wyckoffRangeResistance - wyckoffRangeSupport, safeAtr) : safeAtr
float wyckoffRangeTolerance = math.max(safeAtr * 0.35, wyckoffRangeHeight * 0.20)
float confirmedPivotAtr = nz(safeAtr[pivotBars], safeAtr)
bool pivotVolumeQuieterThanClimax = volumeAvailable[pivotBars] and not na(wyckoffClimaxRvol) and relativeVolume[pivotBars] <= math.min(1.10, wyckoffClimaxRvol * 0.80)
bool pivotSpreadQuieterThanClimax = not na(relativeSpread[pivotBars]) and not na(wyckoffClimaxSpread) and relativeSpread[pivotBars] <= math.min(1.0, wyckoffClimaxSpread * 0.80)
// Compare a confirmed pivot with the ATR that existed at that pivot, not with
// the current bar's ATR which may have changed materially during confirmation.
bool accumulationAutomaticRallyEvent = wyckoffMode == 1 and wyckoffStage == 2 and newPivotHigh and confirmedPivotBar > wyckoffClimaxBar and pivotHigh - wyckoffRangeSupport >= confirmedPivotAtr
bool distributionAutomaticReactionEvent = wyckoffMode == -1 and wyckoffStage == 2 and newPivotLow and confirmedPivotBar > wyckoffClimaxBar and wyckoffRangeResistance - pivotLow >= confirmedPivotAtr
bool accumulationSecondaryTestEvent = wyckoffMode == 1 and wyckoffStage >= 3 and wyckoffStage <= 4 and newPivotLow and confirmedPivotBar > wyckoffAutomaticBar and math.abs(pivotLow - wyckoffRangeSupport) <= wyckoffRangeTolerance and pivotVolumeQuieterThanClimax and pivotSpreadQuieterThanClimax
bool distributionSecondaryTestEvent = wyckoffMode == -1 and wyckoffStage >= 3 and wyckoffStage <= 4 and newPivotHigh and confirmedPivotBar > wyckoffAutomaticBar and math.abs(pivotHigh - wyckoffRangeResistance) <= wyckoffRangeTolerance and pivotVolumeQuieterThanClimax and pivotSpreadQuieterThanClimax
// Spring/UTAD belong to the active Wyckoff range. Do not require the latest
// generic swing to be the same level, because that coupling can miss a valid
// range-boundary sweep. A bar sweeping both range sides is ambiguous and is
// excluded from directional Phase C classification.
bool wyckoffTwoSidedRangeSweep = wyckoffMode != 0 and not na(wyckoffRangeSupport) and not na(wyckoffRangeResistance) and low < wyckoffRangeSupport - breakBuffer and high > wyckoffRangeResistance + breakBuffer
float wyckoffMaximumSweepPenetration = math.max(safeAtr * 1.25, wyckoffRangeHeight * 0.35)
float wyckoffSpringPenetration = not na(wyckoffRangeSupport) ? wyckoffRangeSupport - low : na
float wyckoffUtadPenetration = not na(wyckoffRangeResistance) ? high - wyckoffRangeResistance : na
bool sameBarSpringRaw = barstate.isconfirmed and wyckoffMode == 1 and wyckoffStage >= 4 and wyckoffStage < 6 and not wyckoffTwoSidedRangeSweep and not na(wyckoffRangeSupport) and wyckoffSpringPenetration >= breakBuffer and wyckoffSpringPenetration <= wyckoffMaximumSweepPenetration and close > wyckoffRangeSupport + breakHoldBuffer
bool sameBarUtadRaw = barstate.isconfirmed and wyckoffMode == -1 and wyckoffStage >= 4 and wyckoffStage < 6 and not wyckoffTwoSidedRangeSweep and not na(wyckoffRangeResistance) and wyckoffUtadPenetration >= breakBuffer and wyckoffUtadPenetration <= wyckoffMaximumSweepPenetration and close < wyckoffRangeResistance - breakHoldBuffer
bool sameBarSpringQualified = sameBarSpringRaw and closeLocation >= 0.55 and (breakLowerWickShare >= 0.30 or close > open and bodyRatio >= 0.30)
bool sameBarUtadQualified = sameBarUtadRaw and closeLocation <= 0.45 and (breakUpperWickShare >= 0.30 or close < open and bodyRatio >= 0.30)
int barsFromWyckoffPhaseCSweep = na(wyckoffPhaseCSweepBar) ? 100000 : bar_index - wyckoffPhaseCSweepBar
bool insideWyckoffPhaseCTestWindow = wyckoffPhaseCSweepDirection != 0 and barsFromWyckoffPhaseCSweep > 0 and barsFromWyckoffPhaseCSweep <= retestWindow
bool accumulationSpringTestEvent = barstate.isconfirmed and wyckoffMode == 1 and wyckoffStage == 5 and insideWyckoffPhaseCTestWindow and wyckoffPhaseCSweepDirection == 1 and low <= wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel + retestTolerance and low > wyckoffPhaseCSweepExtreme and close > wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel and close > open and quieterTestVolume and quieterTestSpread
bool distributionUtadTestEvent = barstate.isconfirmed and wyckoffMode == -1 and wyckoffStage == 5 and insideWyckoffPhaseCTestWindow and wyckoffPhaseCSweepDirection == -1 and high >= wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel - retestTolerance and high < wyckoffPhaseCSweepExtreme and close < wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel and close < open and quieterTestVolume and quieterTestSpread
bool wyckoffResistanceAttemptRaw = f_level_break_attempt(true, wyckoffRangeResistance, nz(wyckoffRangeResistance[1], wyckoffRangeResistance), breakBuffer)
bool wyckoffSupportAttemptRaw = f_level_break_attempt(false, wyckoffRangeSupport, nz(wyckoffRangeSupport[1], wyckoffRangeSupport), breakBuffer)
bool wyckoffResistanceAttempt = wyckoffMode != 0 and not na(wyckoffRangeResistance) and wyckoffResistanceAttemptRaw
bool wyckoffSupportAttempt = wyckoffMode != 0 and not na(wyckoffRangeSupport) and wyckoffSupportAttemptRaw
var int wyckoffRangeAttemptDirection = 0
var int wyckoffRangeAttemptMode = 0
var float wyckoffRangeAttemptLevel = na
var float wyckoffRangeAttemptExtreme = na
var int wyckoffRangeAttemptBar = na
int wyckoffRangeAttemptAge = na(wyckoffRangeAttemptBar) ? 100000 : bar_index - wyckoffRangeAttemptBar
bool delayedSpringQualified = barstate.isconfirmed and wyckoffMode == 1 and wyckoffStage >= 4 and wyckoffStage < 6 and wyckoffRangeAttemptMode == wyckoffMode and wyckoffRangeAttemptDirection == -1 and wyckoffRangeAttemptAge > 0 and wyckoffRangeAttemptAge <= fakeoutWindow and close > wyckoffRangeAttemptLevel + breakHoldBuffer and closeLocation >= 0.55 and low > wyckoffRangeAttemptExtreme and (breakLowerWickShare >= 0.20 or close > open)
bool delayedUtadQualified = barstate.isconfirmed and wyckoffMode == -1 and wyckoffStage >= 4 and wyckoffStage < 6 and wyckoffRangeAttemptMode == wyckoffMode and wyckoffRangeAttemptDirection == 1 and wyckoffRangeAttemptAge > 0 and wyckoffRangeAttemptAge <= fakeoutWindow and close < wyckoffRangeAttemptLevel - breakHoldBuffer and closeLocation <= 0.45 and high < wyckoffRangeAttemptExtreme and (breakUpperWickShare >= 0.20 or close < open)
bool accumulationSpringEvent = sameBarSpringQualified or delayedSpringQualified
bool distributionUtadEvent = sameBarUtadQualified or delayedUtadQualified
bool wyckoffResistanceBreakEligible = wyckoffMode == 1 and wyckoffStage >= 4 and wyckoffStage < 6 and not wyckoffResistanceWasBroken and not na(wyckoffRangeResistance)
bool wyckoffSupportBreakEligible = wyckoffMode == -1 and wyckoffStage >= 4 and wyckoffStage < 6 and not wyckoffSupportWasBroken and not na(wyckoffRangeSupport)
[_, wyckoffResistanceCrossRaw, _, _, _, _] = f_static_break_state(14, true, wyckoffResistanceBreakEligible ? wyckoffRangeResistance : na, wyckoffRangeSupport, breakBuffer, bullishExceptionalBreakBar)
[_, wyckoffSupportCrossRaw, _, _, _, _] = f_static_break_state(15, false, wyckoffSupportBreakEligible ? wyckoffRangeSupport : na, wyckoffRangeResistance, breakBuffer, bearishExceptionalBreakBar)
bool wyckoffResistanceCrossEvent = wyckoffResistanceBreakEligible and wyckoffResistanceCrossRaw
bool wyckoffSupportCrossEvent = wyckoffSupportBreakEligible and wyckoffSupportCrossRaw
bool clearWyckoffRangeAttempt = delayedSpringQualified or delayedUtadQualified or wyckoffRangeAttemptAge > fakeoutWindow or wyckoffMode == 0 or wyckoffRangeAttemptMode != wyckoffMode or wyckoffResistanceCrossEvent or wyckoffSupportCrossEvent
if clearWyckoffRangeAttempt
    wyckoffRangeAttemptDirection := 0
    wyckoffRangeAttemptMode := 0
    wyckoffRangeAttemptLevel := na
    wyckoffRangeAttemptExtreme := na
    wyckoffRangeAttemptBar := na
if wyckoffResistanceAttempt
    wyckoffRangeAttemptDirection := 1
    wyckoffRangeAttemptMode := wyckoffMode
    wyckoffRangeAttemptLevel := wyckoffRangeResistance
    wyckoffRangeAttemptExtreme := high
    wyckoffRangeAttemptBar := bar_index
else if wyckoffSupportAttempt
    wyckoffRangeAttemptDirection := -1
    wyckoffRangeAttemptMode := wyckoffMode
    wyckoffRangeAttemptLevel := wyckoffRangeSupport
    wyckoffRangeAttemptExtreme := low
    wyckoffRangeAttemptBar := bar_index
bool wyckoffResistanceBreak = wyckoffResistanceCrossEvent and bullishBreakCandleQuality
bool wyckoffSupportBreak = wyckoffSupportCrossEvent and bearishBreakCandleQuality
bool accumulationSosEvent = wyckoffResistanceBreak and volumeAvailable and standardVolumeEffort and vsaWideResult and closeLocation >= 0.60
bool distributionSowEvent = wyckoffSupportBreak and volumeAvailable and standardVolumeEffort and vsaWideResult and closeLocation <= 0.40
bool quietWyckoffBackupPivot = volumeAvailable[pivotBars] and relativeVolume[pivotBars] <= 1.10 and not na(relativeSpread[pivotBars]) and relativeSpread[pivotBars] <= 1.0
bool accumulationLpsEvent = wyckoffMode == 1 and wyckoffStage >= 6 and wyckoffStage <= 7 and newPivotLow and not na(wyckoffSignBar) and confirmedPivotBar > wyckoffSignBar and quietWyckoffBackupPivot and not na(wyckoffRangeResistance) and math.abs(pivotLow - wyckoffRangeResistance) <= wyckoffRangeTolerance and close[pivotBars] > wyckoffRangeResistance and closeLocation[pivotBars] >= 0.55
bool distributionLpsyEvent = wyckoffMode == -1 and wyckoffStage >= 6 and wyckoffStage <= 7 and newPivotHigh and not na(wyckoffSignBar) and confirmedPivotBar > wyckoffSignBar and quietWyckoffBackupPivot and not na(wyckoffRangeSupport) and math.abs(pivotHigh - wyckoffRangeSupport) <= wyckoffRangeTolerance and close[pivotBars] < wyckoffRangeSupport and closeLocation[pivotBars] <= 0.45
bool accumulationPhaseEEvent = barstate.isconfirmed and wyckoffMode == 1 and wyckoffStage == 7 and wyckoffPhase < 5 and not na(wyckoffRangeResistance) and close > wyckoffRangeResistance + safeAtr and sma24 > sma24[1]
bool distributionPhaseEEvent = barstate.isconfirmed and wyckoffMode == -1 and wyckoffStage == 7 and wyckoffPhase < 5 and not na(wyckoffRangeSupport) and close < wyckoffRangeSupport - safeAtr and sma24 < sma24[1]

if wyckoffPhaseCSweepDirection != 0 and barsFromWyckoffPhaseCSweep > retestWindow
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepDirection := 0
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel := na
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepExtreme := na
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepBar := na

if sellingClimaxEvent
    int accumulationStartBar = wyckoffMode == 1 and not na(wyckoffSequenceStartBar) ? wyckoffSequenceStartBar : bar_index
    wyckoffMode := 1
    wyckoffStage := 2
    wyckoffPhase := 1
    wyckoffSequenceStartBar := accumulationStartBar
    wyckoffClimaxBar := bar_index
    wyckoffAutomaticBar := na
    wyckoffPhaseEBar := na
    wyckoffRangeSupport := low
    wyckoffRangeResistance := na
    wyckoffClimaxRvol := relativeVolume
    wyckoffClimaxSpread := relativeSpread
    wyckoffResistanceWasBroken := false
    wyckoffSupportWasBroken := false
else if buyingClimaxEvent
    int distributionStartBar = wyckoffMode == -1 and not na(wyckoffSequenceStartBar) ? wyckoffSequenceStartBar : bar_index
    wyckoffMode := -1
    wyckoffStage := 2
    wyckoffPhase := 1
    wyckoffSequenceStartBar := distributionStartBar
    wyckoffClimaxBar := bar_index
    wyckoffAutomaticBar := na
    wyckoffPhaseEBar := na
    wyckoffRangeSupport := na
    wyckoffRangeResistance := high
    wyckoffClimaxRvol := relativeVolume
    wyckoffClimaxSpread := relativeSpread
    wyckoffResistanceWasBroken := false
    wyckoffSupportWasBroken := false
else if preliminarySupportEvent
    wyckoffMode := 1
    wyckoffStage := 1
    wyckoffPhase := 1
    wyckoffSequenceStartBar := bar_index
    wyckoffRangeSupport := low
else if preliminarySupplyEvent
    wyckoffMode := -1
    wyckoffStage := 1
    wyckoffPhase := 1
    wyckoffSequenceStartBar := bar_index
    wyckoffRangeResistance := high
else if accumulationAutomaticRallyEvent
    wyckoffStage := 3
    wyckoffPhase := 1
    wyckoffAutomaticBar := confirmedPivotBar
    wyckoffRangeResistance := pivotHigh
    wyckoffResistanceWasBroken := false
else if distributionAutomaticReactionEvent
    wyckoffStage := 3
    wyckoffPhase := 1
    wyckoffAutomaticBar := confirmedPivotBar
    wyckoffRangeSupport := pivotLow
    wyckoffSupportWasBroken := false
else if accumulationSecondaryTestEvent
    wyckoffStage := 4
    wyckoffPhase := 2
    wyckoffRangeSupport := math.min(wyckoffRangeSupport, pivotLow)
else if distributionSecondaryTestEvent
    wyckoffStage := 4
    wyckoffPhase := 2
    wyckoffRangeResistance := math.max(wyckoffRangeResistance, pivotHigh)
else if accumulationSpringEvent
    wyckoffStage := 5
    wyckoffPhase := 3
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepDirection := 1
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel := wyckoffRangeSupport
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepExtreme := low
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepBar := bar_index
else if accumulationSpringTestEvent
    wyckoffStage := 5
    wyckoffPhase := 3
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepDirection := 0
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel := na
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepExtreme := na
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepBar := na
else if distributionUtadEvent
    wyckoffStage := 5
    wyckoffPhase := 3
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepDirection := -1
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel := wyckoffRangeResistance
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepExtreme := high
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepBar := bar_index
else if distributionUtadTestEvent
    wyckoffStage := 5
    wyckoffPhase := 3
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepDirection := 0
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel := na
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepExtreme := na
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepBar := na
else if accumulationSosEvent
    wyckoffStage := 6
    wyckoffPhase := 4
    wyckoffSignBar := bar_index
    wyckoffResistanceWasBroken := true
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepDirection := 0
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel := na
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepExtreme := na
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepBar := na
else if distributionSowEvent
    wyckoffStage := 6
    wyckoffPhase := 4
    wyckoffSignBar := bar_index
    wyckoffSupportWasBroken := true
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepDirection := 0
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel := na
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepExtreme := na
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepBar := na
else if accumulationLpsEvent
    wyckoffStage := 7
    wyckoffPhase := 4
else if distributionLpsyEvent
    wyckoffStage := 7
    wyckoffPhase := 4
else if accumulationPhaseEEvent or distributionPhaseEEvent
    wyckoffStage := 8
    wyckoffPhase := 5
    wyckoffPhaseEBar := bar_index

bool wyckoffPhaseERecent = wyckoffPhase == 5 and not na(wyckoffPhaseEBar) and bar_index - wyckoffPhaseEBar <= contextLookback
int wyckoffContextSignal = wyckoffMode == 1 and (wyckoffPhase == 3 or wyckoffPhase == 4 or wyckoffPhaseERecent) ? 1 : wyckoffMode == -1 and (wyckoffPhase == 3 or wyckoffPhase == 4 or wyckoffPhaseERecent) ? -1 : 0


bool wyckoffRangeReady = wyckoffMode != 0 and not na(wyckoffRangeSupport) and not na(wyckoffRangeResistance) and not na(wyckoffSequenceStartBar)
// failed breaks. They share one capped auction/trigger vote later, but the
// dashboard must not label every generic sweep as a Wyckoff event.
int barsSinceBullishWyckoff = ta.barssince(accumulationSpringEvent or accumulationSpringTestEvent or accumulationSosEvent or accumulationLpsEvent or accumulationPhaseEEvent)
int barsSinceBearishWyckoff = ta.barssince(distributionUtadEvent or distributionUtadTestEvent or distributionSowEvent or distributionLpsyEvent or distributionPhaseEEvent)
int barsSinceBullishLiquidityBehavior = ta.barssince(bullishSweep or bearishBreakFailed or bullishWyckoffTest)
int barsSinceBearishLiquidityBehavior = ta.barssince(bearishSweep or bullishBreakFailed or bearishWyckoffTest)
int triggerEvidenceLookback = math.min(contextLookback, math.max(4, retestWindow))
bool recentBullishAuctionTrigger = (not na(barsSinceBullishWyckoff) and barsSinceBullishWyckoff <= triggerEvidenceLookback) or (not na(barsSinceBullishLiquidityBehavior) and barsSinceBullishLiquidityBehavior <= triggerEvidenceLookback)
bool recentBearishAuctionTrigger = (not na(barsSinceBearishWyckoff) and barsSinceBearishWyckoff <= triggerEvidenceLookback) or (not na(barsSinceBearishLiquidityBehavior) and barsSinceBearishLiquidityBehavior <= triggerEvidenceLookback)

// Three-bar imbalance is location/retest context, never a standalone entry.
[bullishFvgActive, bearishFvgActive, bullishFvgBottom, bullishFvgTop, bearishFvgBottom, bearishFvgTop, bullishFvgRetestRaw, bearishFvgRetestRaw] = f_update_fvg_context(safeAtr, volumeAvailable, relativeVolume, standardVolumeEffort, chartSeconds, math.max(24, retestWindow * 4))
bool fvgConflict = bullishFvgActive and bearishFvgActive and math.max(bullishFvgBottom, bearishFvgBottom) <= math.min(bullishFvgTop, bearishFvgTop)
bool bullishFvgRetest = bullishFvgRetestRaw and not fvgConflict
bool bearishFvgRetest = bearishFvgRetestRaw and not fvgConflict
bool bullishFvgLocation = bullishFvgActive and not fvgConflict and close >= bullishFvgBottom - safeAtr * 0.15 and close <= bullishFvgTop + safeAtr * 0.50
bool bearishFvgLocation = bearishFvgActive and not fvgConflict and close <= bearishFvgTop + safeAtr * 0.15 and close >= bearishFvgBottom - safeAtr * 0.50

// Candle behavior restored from V62/V78 in compressed classes. Names do not
// create separate votes: rejection/reversal/compression/displacement are one behavior axis.
float realBody = math.max(math.abs(close - open), syminfo.mintick)
float upperWick = high - math.max(open, close)
float lowerWick = math.min(open, close) - low
bool precedingDownContext = resolvedStructureBias[1] == -1 or (close[1] < sma24[1] and sma24[1] <= sma50[1])
bool precedingUpContext = resolvedStructureBias[1] == 1 or (close[1] > sma24[1] and sma24[1] >= sma50[1])
bool bullishEngulfingPattern = precedingDownContext and close > open and close[1] < open[1] and open <= close[1] and close >= open[1] and bodyRatio >= 0.50
bool bearishEngulfingPattern = precedingUpContext and close < open and close[1] > open[1] and open >= close[1] and close <= open[1] and bodyRatio >= 0.50
bool bullishPinPattern = precedingDownContext and bodyRatio <= 0.45 and lowerWick >= realBody * 2.0 and upperWick <= realBody * 1.25
bool bearishPinPattern = precedingUpContext and bodyRatio <= 0.45 and upperWick >= realBody * 2.0 and lowerWick <= realBody * 1.25
bool bullishReversalPatternRaw = bullishEngulfingPattern or bullishPinPattern
bool bearishReversalPatternRaw = bearishEngulfingPattern or bearishPinPattern
f_price_action_breakouts() =>
    float rangeHigh = ta.highest(high[1], sidewaysRangeBars)
    float rangeLow = ta.lowest(low[1], sidewaysRangeBars)
    bool rangeReady = usePriceActionBreakouts and rangeHigh > rangeLow + syminfo.mintick and rangeHigh - rangeLow <= nz(safeAtr[1], safeAtr) * sidewaysRangeAtrWidth
    [_, bullBreak, bullLevel, bullEdge, _, _] = f_static_break_state(12, true, rangeReady ? rangeHigh : na, rangeLow, breakBuffer, bullishExceptionalBreakBar)
    [_, bearBreak, bearLevel, bearEdge, _, _] = f_static_break_state(13, false, rangeReady ? rangeLow : na, rangeHigh, breakBuffer, bearishExceptionalBreakBar)
    [rangeHigh, rangeLow, rangeReady, bullBreak, bearBreak, bullBreak and bullishBreakCandleQuality, bearBreak and bearishBreakCandleQuality, bullLevel, bullEdge, bearLevel, bearEdge]

[sidewaysRangeHigh, sidewaysRangeLow, sidewaysRangeReady, bullishRangeBreakRaw, bearishRangeBreakRaw, bullishRangeBreakAfterDecline, bearishRangeBreakAfterAdvance, bullishRangeBreakLevel, bullishRangeBreakEdge, bearishRangeBreakLevel, bearishRangeBreakEdge] = f_price_action_breakouts()
float compressionUpperNow = convergingImportantStructure ? f_project(previousImportantHigh, previousImportantHighBar, lastImportantHigh, lastImportantHighBar, bar_index) : na
float compressionLowerNow = convergingImportantStructure ? f_project(previousImportantLow, previousImportantLowBar, lastImportantLow, lastImportantLowBar, bar_index) : na
bool compressionBreakUpRaw = f_level_break_event(true, compressionUpperNow, nz(compressionUpperNow[1], compressionUpperNow), breakBuffer, bullishExceptionalBreakBar)
bool compressionBreakDownRaw = f_level_break_event(false, compressionLowerNow, nz(compressionLowerNow[1], compressionLowerNow), breakBuffer, bearishExceptionalBreakBar)
bool compressionBreakContext = barstate.isconfirmed and useChartPatterns and convergingImportantStructure and not newImportantHighAnchor and not newImportantLowAnchor and compressionUpperNow > compressionLowerNow + syminfo.mintick
int compressionBreakDirection = not compressionBreakContext ? 0 : compressionBreakUpRaw ? 1 : compressionBreakDownRaw ? -1 : 0
sidewaysRangeHigh := compressionBreakDirection != 0 ? compressionUpperNow : sidewaysRangeHigh
sidewaysRangeLow := compressionBreakDirection != 0 ? compressionLowerNow : sidewaysRangeLow
bullishRangeBreakLevel := compressionBreakDirection == 1 ? compressionUpperNow : bullishRangeBreakLevel
bullishRangeBreakEdge := compressionBreakDirection == 1 ? compressionLowerNow : bullishRangeBreakEdge
bearishRangeBreakLevel := compressionBreakDirection == -1 ? compressionLowerNow : bearishRangeBreakLevel
bearishRangeBreakEdge := compressionBreakDirection == -1 ? compressionUpperNow : bearishRangeBreakEdge
bullishRangeBreakRaw := bullishRangeBreakRaw or compressionBreakDirection == 1
bearishRangeBreakRaw := bearishRangeBreakRaw or compressionBreakDirection == -1
bullishRangeBreakAfterDecline := bullishRangeBreakRaw and bullishBreakCandleQuality and volumePass
bearishRangeBreakAfterAdvance := bearishRangeBreakRaw and bearishBreakCandleQuality and volumePass
bool bullishCandleConfirmation = useCandleConfirmation and barstate.isconfirmed and bullishReversalPatternRaw[1] and close > high[1]
bool bearishCandleConfirmation = useCandleConfirmation and barstate.isconfirmed and bearishReversalPatternRaw[1] and close < low[1]
bool bullishCandleImportantContext = bullishWyckoffTest or bullishSweep or bearishBreakFailed or bullishDemandRetest or bullishRetest or bullishChoch or bullishBos or bullishRangeBreakAfterDecline or accumulationSpringEvent or accumulationSpringTestEvent or accumulationSosEvent or accumulationLpsEvent or accumulationPhaseEEvent
bool bearishCandleImportantContext = bearishWyckoffTest or bearishSweep or bullishBreakFailed or bearishSupplyRetest or bearishRetest or bearishChoch or bearishBos or bearishRangeBreakAfterAdvance or distributionUtadEvent or distributionUtadTestEvent or distributionSowEvent or distributionLpsyEvent or distributionPhaseEEvent
bool bullishImportantCandleConfirmation = bullishCandleConfirmation and (bullishCandleImportantContext or bullishCandleImportantContext[1])
bool bearishImportantCandleConfirmation = bearishCandleConfirmation and (bearishCandleImportantContext or bearishCandleImportantContext[1])
int barsSinceBullishCandle = ta.barssince(bullishCandleConfirmation)
int barsSinceBearishCandle = ta.barssince(bearishCandleConfirmation)
bool recentBullishCandle = not na(barsSinceBullishCandle) and barsSinceBullishCandle <= triggerEvidenceLookback
bool recentBearishCandle = not na(barsSinceBearishCandle) and barsSinceBearishCandle <= triggerEvidenceLookback
f_update_candle_volume_vsa_state() =>
    var float lastKeyCandleHigh = na
    var float lastKeyCandleLow = na
    var int lastKeyCandleBar = na
    var float highVolumeResistanceHigh = na
    var float highVolumeResistanceLow = na
    var int highVolumeResistanceBar = na
    var float highVolumeSupportHigh = na
    var float highVolumeSupportLow = na
    var int highVolumeSupportBar = na
    bool bullishHorizontalRetestSetup = false
    bool bearishHorizontalRetestSetup = false
    int vsaSignal = 0
    // وصف آخر شمعة مغلقة: مختصر، ولا يمثل دخولًا منفردًا.
    bool strongBullCandle = close > open and bodyRatio >= 0.70
    bool strongBearCandle = close < open and bodyRatio >= 0.70
    bullishHorizontalRetestSetup := barstate.isconfirmed and resistanceClusterActsAsSupport and (bullishCandleConfirmation or strongBullCandle)
    bearishHorizontalRetestSetup := barstate.isconfirmed and supportClusterActsAsResistance and (bearishCandleConfirmation or strongBearCandle)
    bool highVolumeCandle = volumeAvailable and (relativeVolume >= volumeMultiple or heatmapVolumeHighOrHigher)
    bool keyRangeCandleNow = barstate.isconfirmed and (vsaWideResult and (strongBullCandle or strongBearCandle) or highVolumeCandle and (vsaWideResult or vsaNarrowResult))
    if keyRangeCandleNow
        lastKeyCandleHigh := high
        lastKeyCandleLow := low
        lastKeyCandleBar := bar_index
    else if not na(lastKeyCandleBar) and bar_index - lastKeyCandleBar > keyCandleMaximumAge
        lastKeyCandleHigh := na
        lastKeyCandleLow := na
        lastKeyCandleBar := na

    bool highVolumePeakZoneEvent = barstate.isconfirmed and highVolumeCandle and precedingUpContext and (nearStrongResistance or high >= nz(lastSwingHigh, high) - safeAtr * 0.25) and (closeLocation <= 0.55 or upperWick >= realBody)
    bool highVolumeBottomZoneEvent = barstate.isconfirmed and highVolumeCandle and precedingDownContext and (nearStrongSupport or low <= nz(lastSwingLow, low) + safeAtr * 0.25) and (closeLocation >= 0.45 or lowerWick >= realBody)
    bool highVolumeResistanceBreakRaw = f_confirmed_level_break(true, highVolumeResistanceHigh, nz(highVolumeResistanceHigh[1], highVolumeResistanceHigh), breakBuffer, bullishExceptionalBreakBar)
    bool highVolumeSupportBreakRaw = f_confirmed_level_break(false, highVolumeSupportLow, nz(highVolumeSupportLow[1], highVolumeSupportLow), breakBuffer, bearishExceptionalBreakBar)
    bool highVolumeResistanceExpired = not na(highVolumeResistanceBar) and (bar_index - highVolumeResistanceBar > keyCandleMaximumAge or highVolumeResistanceBreakRaw)
    bool highVolumeSupportExpired = not na(highVolumeSupportBar) and (bar_index - highVolumeSupportBar > keyCandleMaximumAge or highVolumeSupportBreakRaw)
    if highVolumeResistanceExpired
        highVolumeResistanceHigh := na
        highVolumeResistanceLow := na
        highVolumeResistanceBar := na
    if highVolumeSupportExpired
        highVolumeSupportHigh := na
        highVolumeSupportLow := na
        highVolumeSupportBar := na
    if highVolumePeakZoneEvent
        highVolumeResistanceHigh := high
        highVolumeResistanceLow := low
        highVolumeResistanceBar := bar_index
    if highVolumeBottomZoneEvent
        highVolumeSupportHigh := high
        highVolumeSupportLow := low
        highVolumeSupportBar := bar_index
    bool bullishVsaAbsorption = vsaHighEffort and vsaNarrowResult and closeLocation >= 0.55 and (bullishSweep or bearishBreakFailed)
    bool bearishVsaAbsorption = vsaHighEffort and vsaNarrowResult and closeLocation <= 0.45 and (bearishSweep or bullishBreakFailed)
    bool bullishVsaConfirmation = useVsaAnalysis and barstate.isconfirmed and vsaHighEffort[1] and vsaNarrowResult[1] and close > high[1] and (resolvedStructureBias[1] == -1 or bullishSweep[1] or bearishBreakFailed[1])
    bool bearishVsaConfirmation = useVsaAnalysis and barstate.isconfirmed and vsaHighEffort[1] and vsaNarrowResult[1] and close < low[1] and (resolvedStructureBias[1] == 1 or bearishSweep[1] or bullishBreakFailed[1])
    vsaSignal := not useVsaAnalysis ? 0 : (bullishVsaConfirmation or bullishVsaAbsorption or bullishVsaEffortResult) ? 1 : (bearishVsaConfirmation or bearishVsaAbsorption or bearishVsaEffortResult) ? -1 : 0

    [lastKeyCandleHigh, lastKeyCandleLow, lastKeyCandleBar, highVolumeResistanceHigh, highVolumeResistanceLow, highVolumeResistanceBar, highVolumeSupportHigh, highVolumeSupportLow, highVolumeSupportBar, bullishHorizontalRetestSetup, bearishHorizontalRetestSetup, vsaSignal]

[lastKeyCandleHigh, lastKeyCandleLow, lastKeyCandleBar, highVolumeResistanceHigh, highVolumeResistanceLow, highVolumeResistanceBar, highVolumeSupportHigh, highVolumeSupportLow, highVolumeSupportBar, bullishHorizontalRetestSetup, bearishHorizontalRetestSetup, vsaSignal] = f_update_candle_volume_vsa_state()

// Event-anchored VWAP from V78. It is location/context evidence only, never a hard entry gate.
// Spot FX/indices/CFDs do not fabricate trusted volume for this calculation.
bool structuralVwapAnchorEvent = barstate.isconfirmed and (bullishChoch or bearishChoch or bullishSweep or bearishSweep or bullishBreakFailed or bearishBreakFailed or bullishWyckoffTest or bearishWyckoffTest or accumulationSosEvent or distributionSowEvent or accumulationLpsEvent or distributionLpsyEvent or bullishDemandRetest or bearishSupplyRetest or qualifiedUpperChannelFakeout or qualifiedLowerChannelFakeout or strongResistanceBreak or strongSupportBreak or bullishStructuralPatternBreak or bearishStructuralPatternBreak or bullishRangeBreakAfterDecline or bearishRangeBreakAfterAdvance)
[structuralVwapReady, structuralVwapValue] = f_update_structural_vwap(volumeAvailable, structuralVwapAnchorEvent)
float structuralVwapSlopeTolerance = safeAtr * 0.01
bool structuralVwapNear = structuralVwapReady and not structuralVwapAnchorEvent and math.abs(close - structuralVwapValue) <= safeAtr * 1.50
float structuralVwapSideBuffer = math.max(syminfo.mintick, safeAtr * 0.02)
bool bullishStructuralVwapLocation = structuralVwapNear and close >= structuralVwapValue + structuralVwapSideBuffer and (na(structuralVwapValue[1]) or structuralVwapValue >= structuralVwapValue[1] - structuralVwapSlopeTolerance)
bool bearishStructuralVwapLocation = structuralVwapNear and close <= structuralVwapValue - structuralVwapSideBuffer and (na(structuralVwapValue[1]) or structuralVwapValue <= structuralVwapValue[1] + structuralVwapSlopeTolerance)


// Preserve the exact broken level before the break watcher is reset below.
float confirmedRetestLevel = bullishTrendlineRetest or bearishTrendlineRetest ? watchedBreakBoundaryNow : bullishRetest or bearishRetest ? resolvedBreakEventLevel : na
// شروحات الأحداث العامة والشموع تُعرض في اللوحة الثابتة؛ لا توضع نصوص طويلة فوق السعر.
// =============================================================================
// Latest trade plan only
// =============================================================================
// The trigger snapshots confirmed breaks, true bounces, retests and trend changes before participation filters.
const int SETUP_EVENT_NONE = 0
const int SETUP_EVENT_EARLY = 1
const int SETUP_EVENT_RETEST = 2
const int SETUP_EVENT_BREAK = 3
const int SETUP_SOURCE_OTHER = 0
const int SETUP_SOURCE_AUCTION = 1
const int SETUP_SOURCE_CANDLE = 2
const int SETUP_SOURCE_PATTERN = 3

f_setup_wave_range(bool isLong, int eventBar, int maximumAge, float swingHigh, int swingHighBar, float swingLow, int swingLowBar) =>
    bool validSequence = not na(eventBar) and not na(swingHigh) and not na(swingLow) and not na(swingHighBar) and not na(swingLowBar) and swingHighBar <= eventBar and swingLowBar <= eventBar and eventBar - math.min(swingHighBar, swingLowBar) <= maximumAge and (isLong ? swingLowBar < swingHighBar : swingHighBar < swingLowBar)
    validSequence ? math.abs(swingHigh - swingLow) : na

// ABC/N-wave context is stored with each candidate: A is the prior same-side pivot,
// C is the latest higher-low/lower-high and B is the confirmed reference/broken level.
// N keeps its own B-A range even when Fibonacci later selects a larger principal wave.
type SetupCandidateState
    int dir = 0
    int eventKind = 0
    int priority = 0
    bool momentumExtensionUsed = false
    int eventBar = na
    float ref = na
    float rng = na
    float waveOrigin = na
    float nRange = na
    float stop = na
    int stopKind = 0
    bool usesGuard = false
    float guardLevel = na
    int sourceKind = 0
    bool directBreak = false
    bool reversalEligible = false
    float tolerance = na

var SetupCandidateState longCandidateState = SetupCandidateState.new()
var SetupCandidateState shortCandidateState = SetupCandidateState.new()

f_clear_setup_candidate(SetupCandidateState candidate) =>
    candidate.dir := 0
    candidate.eventKind := SETUP_EVENT_NONE
    candidate.priority := 0
    candidate.momentumExtensionUsed := false
    candidate.eventBar := na
    candidate.ref := na
    candidate.rng := na
    candidate.waveOrigin := na
    candidate.nRange := na
    candidate.stop := na
    candidate.stopKind := 0
    candidate.usesGuard := false
    candidate.guardLevel := na
    candidate.sourceKind := SETUP_SOURCE_OTHER
    candidate.directBreak := false
    candidate.reversalEligible := false
    candidate.tolerance := na
    candidate

type SetupInputContext
    bool firstSessionValue = false
    int chartSecondsValue = 0
    float safeAtrValue = na
    float dynamicAverageValue = na
    int movingAverageBiasValue = 0
    int structureBiasValue = 0
    int higherScoreValue = 0
    int vsaSignalValue = 0
    float closeLocationValue = na
    float bodyRatioValue = na
    int retestWindowValue = 0
    float retestToleranceValue = na
    float breakBufferValue = na
    bool earlyEngineEnabled = false
    bool requireHtfAlignment = false
    bool adaptiveEntryQuality = false
    bool preventExtended = false
    bool volumePassValue = false
    bool bullCandleQuality = false
    bool bearCandleQuality = false
    int bullishTechnicalEventValue = 0
    int bearishTechnicalEventValue = 0
    float bullishTechnicalLevelValue = na
    float bearishTechnicalLevelValue = na
f_retest_event_priority(bool allowed, int subKind, float closeQuality) =>
    allowed ? 280 + (subKind == 4 ? 100 : subKind == 7 or subKind == 8 ? 80 : subKind == 6 ? 65 : subKind > 1 ? 50 : 20) + int(math.round(closeQuality * 10.0)) : 0

f_break_event_priority(bool allowed, int horizontalKind, bool channelBreak, bool trendBreak, bool structureBreak, bool directQuality) =>
    allowed ? 300 + (horizontalKind == 3 ? 80 : horizontalKind == 2 or horizontalKind == 1 ? 70 : horizontalKind == 5 ? 45 : channelBreak ? 55 : trendBreak ? 45 : structureBreak ? 35 : 25) + (directQuality ? 10 : 0) : 0

f_early_event_priority(bool allowed, bool sweep, bool candleSignal) =>
    allowed ? 200 + (sweep ? 45 : candleSignal ? 35 : 15) : 0

f_best_entry_event(int retestPriority, int breakPriority, int earlyPriority) =>
    int priority = math.max(retestPriority, math.max(breakPriority, earlyPriority))
    [priority == 0 ? SETUP_EVENT_NONE : retestPriority == priority ? SETUP_EVENT_RETEST : breakPriority == priority ? SETUP_EVENT_BREAK : SETUP_EVENT_EARLY, priority]

f_update_setup_candidate_state(SetupCandidateState candidate, int directionFilter, SetupInputContext ctx) =>
    if ctx.firstSessionValue
        f_clear_setup_candidate(candidate)
    bool isLong = directionFilter == 1
    int maximumBars = ctx.chartSecondsValue <= 5 * 60 ? math.max(ctx.retestWindowValue, 16) : ctx.chartSecondsValue <= 15 * 60 ? math.max(ctx.retestWindowValue, 12) : ctx.chartSecondsValue <= 60 * 60 ? math.max(ctx.retestWindowValue, 8) : math.max(ctx.retestWindowValue, 6)
    int storedCandidateMaximumBars = candidate.directBreak ? math.max(ctx.retestWindowValue, maximumBars) : maximumBars
    bool structureBreak = isLong ? bullishBreak : bearishBreak
    bool trendBreak = isLong ? bullishTrendlineBreakEvent or bullishSecondaryTrendBreakEvent : bearishTrendlineBreakEvent or bearishSecondaryTrendBreakEvent
    float trendBreakReference = isLong ? (bullishSecondaryTrendBreakEvent ? secondaryFallingTrendNow : watchedBreakBoundaryNow) : (bearishSecondaryTrendBreakEvent ? secondaryRisingTrendNow : watchedBreakBoundaryNow)
    bool channelBreak = isLong ? majorBreakoutUpEvent : majorBreakoutDownEvent
    int technicalEvent = isLong ? ctx.bullishTechnicalEventValue : ctx.bearishTechnicalEventValue
    float technicalLevel = isLong ? ctx.bullishTechnicalLevelValue : ctx.bearishTechnicalLevelValue
    int horizontalKind = isLong ? (strongResistanceBreak ? 3 : historicalResistanceBreak ? 2 : higherResistanceBreak ? 1 : technicalEvent == 1 ? 5 : 0) : (strongSupportBreak ? 3 : historicalSupportBreak ? 2 : higherSupportBreak ? 1 : technicalEvent == 1 ? 5 : 0)
    bool horizontalBreak = horizontalKind != 0
    float horizontalLevel = isLong ? (horizontalKind == 3 ? confirmedResistanceBreakLevel : horizontalKind == 2 ? confirmedHistoricalResistance : horizontalKind == 1 ? confirmedHigherResistance : horizontalKind == 5 ? technicalLevel : na) : (horizontalKind == 3 ? confirmedSupportBreakLevel : horizontalKind == 2 ? confirmedHistoricalSupport : horizontalKind == 1 ? confirmedHigherSupport : horizontalKind == 5 ? technicalLevel : na)
    bool auctionBreak = isLong ? accumulationSosEvent : distributionSowEvent
    bool rangeBreak = isLong ? bullishRangeBreakRaw : bearishRangeBreakRaw
    bool breakEvent = barstate.isconfirmed and analysisSessionPass and (structureBreak or trendBreak or channelBreak or horizontalBreak or auctionBreak or rangeBreak)
    bool microShift = isLong ? close > open and ctx.closeLocationValue >= 0.60 and (close > nz(high[1], high) or close > ctx.dynamicAverageValue and nz(close[1], close) <= nz((ctx[1]).dynamicAverageValue, ctx.dynamicAverageValue)) : close < open and ctx.closeLocationValue <= 0.40 and (close < nz(low[1], low) or close < ctx.dynamicAverageValue and nz(close[1], close) >= nz((ctx[1]).dynamicAverageValue, ctx.dynamicAverageValue))
    float earlyPenetration = math.max(ctx.retestToleranceValue, ctx.safeAtrValue * 0.75)
    bool zoneLocation = isLong ? demandZoneAnalysisReady and low <= demandZoneHigh + ctx.retestToleranceValue and high >= demandZoneLow - ctx.retestToleranceValue and low >= demandZoneLow - earlyPenetration and close >= demandZoneLow - ctx.retestToleranceValue : supplyZoneAnalysisReady and high >= supplyZoneLow - ctx.retestToleranceValue and low <= supplyZoneHigh + ctx.retestToleranceValue and high <= supplyZoneHigh + earlyPenetration and close <= supplyZoneHigh + ctx.retestToleranceValue
    bool breakerLocation = isLong ? bullishBreakerAnalysisReady and low <= bullishBreakerHigh + ctx.retestToleranceValue and high >= bullishBreakerLow - ctx.retestToleranceValue and low >= bullishBreakerLow - earlyPenetration and close >= bullishBreakerLow - ctx.retestToleranceValue : bearishBreakerAnalysisReady and high >= bearishBreakerLow - ctx.retestToleranceValue and low <= bearishBreakerHigh + ctx.retestToleranceValue and high <= bearishBreakerHigh + earlyPenetration and close <= bearishBreakerHigh + ctx.retestToleranceValue
    float pivotLevel = isLong ? lastSwingLow : lastSwingHigh
    bool pivotLocation = not na(pivotLevel) and (isLong ? low <= pivotLevel + ctx.retestToleranceValue and low >= pivotLevel - earlyPenetration and close >= pivotLevel - ctx.retestToleranceValue : high >= pivotLevel - ctx.retestToleranceValue and high <= pivotLevel + earlyPenetration and close <= pivotLevel + ctx.retestToleranceValue)
    bool clusterLocation = isLong ? nearStrongSupport or resistanceClusterActsAsSupport : nearStrongResistance or supportClusterActsAsResistance
    bool earlyLocation = clusterLocation or pivotLocation or not touchingStrongSupportCluster and not touchingStrongResistanceCluster and (zoneLocation or breakerLocation)
    bool sweepEvent = isLong ? bullishSweep : bearishSweep
    bool chochEvent = isLong ? bullishChoch : bearishChoch
    bool wyckoffSweepSeed = isLong ? accumulationSpringEvent : distributionUtadEvent
    bool candleEvent = isLong ? bullishImportantCandleConfirmation : bearishImportantCandleConfirmation
    bool earlyReversalSeed = sweepEvent or chochEvent or wyckoffSweepSeed
    bool earlyReversal = ctx.earlyEngineEnabled and (earlyReversalSeed or microShift and earlyLocation and candleEvent) and ctx.vsaSignalValue != (isLong ? -1 : 1)
    float continuationTolerance = math.max(ctx.retestToleranceValue, ctx.safeAtrValue * 0.20)
    float primaryRetest = f_nearest_price_level(not isLong, close, 0.0, isLong ? (resistanceClusterActsAsSupport ? brokenResistanceHigh : na) : (supportClusterActsAsResistance ? brokenSupportLow : na), isLong ? nearestSupportZoneHigh : nearestResistanceZoneLow, pivotLevel, na, na, na, na, na, na, na)
    float continuationReference = not na(primaryRetest) and math.abs(close - primaryRetest) <= ctx.safeAtrValue ? primaryRetest : f_nearest_price_level(not isLong, close, 0.0, ctx.dynamicAverageValue, isLong ? (demandZoneAnalysisReady ? demandZoneHigh : na) : (supplyZoneAnalysisReady ? supplyZoneLow : na), isLong ? (bullishBreakerAnalysisReady ? bullishBreakerHigh : na) : (bearishBreakerAnalysisReady ? bearishBreakerLow : na), na, na, na, na, na, na, na)
    bool continuationTrend = ctx.structureBiasValue == directionFilter or ctx.structureBiasValue == 0 and ctx.movingAverageBiasValue == directionFilter and (not ctx.requireHtfAlignment or ctx.higherScoreValue * directionFilter >= 0)
    bool continuationRetest = not na(continuationReference) and continuationTrend and (isLong ? low <= continuationReference + continuationTolerance and low >= continuationReference - ctx.safeAtrValue * 0.75 and (na(lastSwingLow) or low > lastSwingLow - ctx.safeAtrValue * 0.15) and microShift and close > continuationReference and ctx.vsaSignalValue != -1 : high >= continuationReference - continuationTolerance and high <= continuationReference + ctx.safeAtrValue * 0.75 and (na(lastSwingHigh) or high < lastSwingHigh + ctx.safeAtrValue * 0.15) and microShift and close < continuationReference and ctx.vsaSignalValue != 1)
    int retestSubKind = isLong ? (bullishHorizontalRetestSetup ? 4 : bullishRetest ? 7 : qualifiedLowerChannelFakeout or qualifiedBearishBreakFailure ? 8 : bullishTrendlineRetest ? 2 : bullishBreakerRetest ? 3 : bullishDemandRetest ? 5 : accumulationSpringTestEvent or bullishWyckoffTest or accumulationLpsEvent ? 6 : technicalEvent == 2 ? 9 : continuationRetest ? 1 : 0) : (bearishHorizontalRetestSetup ? 4 : bearishRetest ? 7 : qualifiedUpperChannelFakeout or qualifiedBullishBreakFailure ? 8 : bearishTrendlineRetest ? 2 : bearishBreakerRetest ? 3 : bearishSupplyRetest ? 5 : distributionUtadTestEvent or bearishWyckoffTest or distributionLpsyEvent ? 6 : technicalEvent == 2 ? 9 : continuationRetest ? 1 : 0)
    int breakSubKind = horizontalBreak ? 4 : trendBreak ? 2 : channelBreak ? 3 : auctionBreak ? 6 : rangeBreak ? 7 : structureBreak ? 1 : 0
    int earlySubKind = wyckoffSweepSeed ? 6 : chochEvent ? 5 : sweepEvent ? 1 : candleEvent ? 2 : 0
    float closeQuality = isLong ? ctx.closeLocationValue : 1.0 - ctx.closeLocationValue
    // Every breakEvent below is already a frozen-level confirmed event (two closes
    // or one exceptional bar). Do not require a simultaneous swing break merely
    // to qualify a horizontal, trend, channel, pattern, range, or Wyckoff break.
    bool directQuality = isLong ? ctx.bullCandleQuality and ctx.closeLocationValue >= 0.55 and sweepUpperWickShare <= 0.35 : ctx.bearCandleQuality and ctx.closeLocationValue <= 0.45 and sweepLowerWickShare <= 0.35
    int retestPriority = f_retest_event_priority(retestSubKind != 0, retestSubKind, closeQuality)
    int breakPriority = f_break_event_priority(breakEvent, horizontalKind, channelBreak, trendBreak, structureBreak, directQuality)
    int earlyPriority = f_early_event_priority(earlyReversal, earlyReversalSeed, candleEvent)
    [selectedEventKind, selectedEventPriority] = f_best_entry_event(retestPriority, breakPriority, earlyPriority)
    int selectedEventSubKind = selectedEventKind == SETUP_EVENT_RETEST ? retestSubKind : selectedEventKind == SETUP_EVENT_BREAK ? breakSubKind : selectedEventKind == SETUP_EVENT_EARLY ? earlySubKind : 0
    bool eventSessionPass = barstate.isconfirmed and analysisSessionPass
    bool eventReady = eventSessionPass and selectedEventKind != SETUP_EVENT_NONE
    float activeRetestTolerance = nz(candidate.tolerance, ctx.retestToleranceValue)
    float candidateInvalidationBuffer = math.max(ctx.breakBufferValue, ctx.safeAtrValue * (candidate.eventKind == SETUP_EVENT_RETEST or candidate.stopKind % 10 == 6 ? 0.15 : 0.10))
    bool expired = candidate.dir != 0 and not na(candidate.eventBar) and bar_index - candidate.eventBar > storedCandidateMaximumBars
    bool stopped = candidate.dir == 1 and not na(candidate.stop) and close < candidate.stop - candidateInvalidationBuffer or candidate.dir == -1 and not na(candidate.stop) and close > candidate.stop + candidateInvalidationBuffer
    bool breakInvalid = candidate.directBreak and not na(candidate.ref) and (candidate.dir == 1 ? close < candidate.ref - activeRetestTolerance * 1.5 : close > candidate.ref + activeRetestTolerance * 1.5)
    if barstate.isconfirmed and (expired or stopped or breakInvalid)
        f_clear_setup_candidate(candidate)
    bool storedRetest = candidate.directBreak and not na(candidate.ref) and (candidate.dir == 1 ? low <= candidate.ref + activeRetestTolerance * 1.5 and close >= candidate.ref - activeRetestTolerance and close > open and ctx.closeLocationValue >= 0.55 and ctx.vsaSignalValue != -1 : candidate.dir == -1 ? high >= candidate.ref - activeRetestTolerance * 1.5 and close <= candidate.ref + activeRetestTolerance and close < open and ctx.closeLocationValue <= 0.45 and ctx.vsaSignalValue != 1 : false)
    if barstate.isconfirmed and storedRetest
        int storedSubKind = selectedEventKind == SETUP_EVENT_RETEST and selectedEventSubKind != 0 ? selectedEventSubKind : 7
        candidate.directBreak := false
        candidate.eventKind := SETUP_EVENT_RETEST
        candidate.priority := math.max(candidate.priority, selectedEventKind == SETUP_EVENT_RETEST ? selectedEventPriority : 390)
        candidate.momentumExtensionUsed := false
        candidate.eventBar := bar_index
        candidate.stop := candidate.dir == 1 ? math.min(nz(candidate.stop, low), low) : math.max(nz(candidate.stop, high), high)
        candidate.stopKind := 20 + storedSubKind
        candidate.usesGuard := not na(candidate.ref)
        candidate.guardLevel := candidate.ref
    if eventReady
        bool earlyNow = selectedEventKind == SETUP_EVENT_EARLY
        bool retestNow = selectedEventKind == SETUP_EVENT_RETEST
        bool directNow = selectedEventKind == SETUP_EVENT_BREAK
        // Confirmation comes from the event engine. Volume remains event-aware
        // evidence later; it is no longer a universal blocker for a two-close break.
        bool primarySrPass = isLong ? horizontalSrSignal != -1 : horizontalSrSignal != 1
        bool majorLevelReversal = directNow and horizontalBreak and not na(horizontalLevel) and (isLong ? close > horizontalLevel : close < horizontalLevel) and directQuality and (not useVolumeFilter or ctx.volumePassValue)
        bool reversalNow = primarySrPass and (isLong ? bullishChoch or accumulationSpringTestEvent or bullishWyckoffTest or qualifiedBearishBreakFailure and ctx.structureBiasValue == 1 or majorLevelReversal : bearishChoch or distributionUtadTestEvent or bearishWyckoffTest or qualifiedBullishBreakFailure and ctx.structureBiasValue == -1 or majorLevelReversal)
        float breakerReference = isLong ? bullishBreakerHigh : bearishBreakerLow
        float horizontalRetestReference = isLong ? brokenResistanceHigh : brokenSupportLow
        float zoneRetestReference = isLong ? demandZoneHigh : supplyZoneLow
        float wyckoffReference = isLong ? (accumulationSpringTestEvent ? wyckoffRangeSupport : bullishWyckoffTest ? wyckoffTestLevel : wyckoffRangeResistance) : (distributionUtadTestEvent ? wyckoffRangeResistance : bearishWyckoffTest ? wyckoffTestLevel : wyckoffRangeSupport)
        float fakeoutReference = isLong ? (qualifiedLowerChannelFakeout ? effectiveChannelFakeoutLevel : resolvedBreakEventLevel) : (qualifiedUpperChannelFakeout ? effectiveChannelFakeoutLevel : resolvedBreakEventLevel)
        float retestReference = selectedEventSubKind == 1 ? continuationReference : selectedEventSubKind == 2 ? watchedBreakBoundaryNow : selectedEventSubKind == 3 ? breakerReference : selectedEventSubKind == 4 ? horizontalRetestReference : selectedEventSubKind == 5 ? zoneRetestReference : selectedEventSubKind == 6 ? wyckoffReference : selectedEventSubKind == 7 ? confirmedRetestLevel : selectedEventSubKind == 8 ? fakeoutReference : selectedEventSubKind == 9 ? technicalLevel : na
        float rangeReference = isLong ? bullishRangeBreakLevel : bearishRangeBreakLevel
        float rangeOppositeEdge = isLong ? bullishRangeBreakEdge : bearishRangeBreakEdge
        float channelReference = isLong ? majorChannelUpperNow : majorChannelLowerNow
        float swingReference = isLong ? confirmedSwingHighBreak : confirmedSwingLowBreak
        float reference = earlyNow ? close : retestNow ? retestReference : horizontalBreak ? horizontalLevel : trendBreak ? trendBreakReference : structureBreak ? swingReference : rangeBreak ? rangeReference : channelBreak ? channelReference : auctionBreak ? (isLong ? wyckoffRangeResistance : wyckoffRangeSupport) : nz(swingReference, close)
        int waveMaximumAge = math.max(maximumBars * 6, pivotBars * 18)
        bool abcWaveReady = not earlyNow and not na(reference) and (isLong ? not na(previousImportantLow) and not na(lastImportantLow) and lastImportantLowBar > previousImportantLowBar and lastImportantLow > previousImportantLow + syminfo.mintick and bar_index - previousImportantLowBar <= waveMaximumAge and reference > math.max(previousImportantLow, lastImportantLow) : not na(previousImportantHigh) and not na(lastImportantHigh) and lastImportantHighBar > previousImportantHighBar and lastImportantHigh < previousImportantHigh - syminfo.mintick and bar_index - previousImportantHighBar <= waveMaximumAge and reference < math.min(previousImportantHigh, lastImportantHigh))
        float abcWaveOrigin = abcWaveReady ? (isLong ? lastImportantLow : lastImportantHigh) : na
        float abcWaveRange = abcWaveReady ? math.abs(reference - (isLong ? previousImportantLow : previousImportantHigh)) : na
        float genericSwingRange = f_setup_wave_range(isLong, bar_index, math.max(maximumBars * 4, pivotBars * 12), lastSwingHigh, lastSwingHighBar, lastSwingLow, lastSwingLowBar)
        float localWaveRange = not na(abcWaveRange) ? abcWaveRange : genericSwingRange
        float retestMeasuredRange = selectedEventSubKind == 3 ? math.abs(isLong ? bullishBreakerHigh - bullishBreakerLow : bearishBreakerHigh - bearishBreakerLow) : selectedEventSubKind == 6 ? math.abs(nz(wyckoffRangeResistance, close) - nz(wyckoffRangeSupport, close)) : localWaveRange
        float baseMeasuredRange = retestNow ? retestMeasuredRange : rangeBreak ? math.abs(rangeReference - rangeOppositeEdge) : channelBreak ? majorChannelWidth : localWaveRange
        bool principalWaveEligible = directNow and (breakSubKind == 1 or breakSubKind == 4 or breakSubKind == 6) and majorChannelBias == directionFilter and not na(majorFirstAnchorPrice) and not na(reference) and (isLong ? reference > majorFirstAnchorPrice : reference < majorFirstAnchorPrice)
        float principalWaveRange = principalWaveEligible ? math.abs(reference - majorFirstAnchorPrice) : na
        float measuredRange = not na(principalWaveRange) ? (not na(baseMeasuredRange) ? math.max(baseMeasuredRange, principalWaveRange) : principalWaveRange) : baseMeasuredRange
        float fallbackDistance = math.max(ctx.safeAtrValue * 1.5, syminfo.mintick * 10.0)
        float baseContextStop = isLong ? nz(lastSwingLow, close - fallbackDistance) : nz(lastSwingHigh, close + fallbackDistance)
        float contextStop = earlyNow ? (isLong ? low : high) : retestNow ? (selectedEventSubKind == 3 ? (isLong ? bullishBreakerLow : bearishBreakerHigh) : (isLong ? low : high)) : baseContextStop
        float specificStop = rangeBreak ? rangeOppositeEdge : trendBreak ? (isLong ? math.min(low, trendBreakReference) : math.max(high, trendBreakReference)) : channelBreak ? (isLong ? math.min(low, majorChannelUpperNow) : math.max(high, majorChannelLowerNow)) : horizontalBreak ? (isLong ? math.min(low, horizontalLevel) : math.max(high, horizontalLevel)) : auctionBreak ? (isLong ? math.min(low, wyckoffRangeResistance) : math.max(high, wyckoffRangeSupport)) : structureBreak ? (isLong ? math.min(low, swingReference) : math.max(high, swingReference)) : na
        bool protectedStop = earlyNow or retestNow
        float stopAnchor = protectedStop ? contextStop : not na(specificStop) ? specificStop : contextStop
        int packedStopKind = (protectedStop ? 2 : not na(specificStop) ? 1 : 0) * 10 + selectedEventSubKind
        bool earlyAuctionSeed = sweepEvent or wyckoffSweepSeed
        int sourceKind = earlyNow ? (earlyAuctionSeed ? SETUP_SOURCE_AUCTION : candleEvent ? SETUP_SOURCE_CANDLE : SETUP_SOURCE_OTHER) : retestNow ? (selectedEventSubKind == 6 or selectedEventSubKind == 8 ? SETUP_SOURCE_AUCTION : SETUP_SOURCE_OTHER) : rangeBreak ? SETUP_SOURCE_PATTERN : auctionBreak ? SETUP_SOURCE_AUCTION : SETUP_SOURCE_OTHER
        bool sameSetup = candidate.dir == directionFilter and candidate.eventKind == selectedEventKind and candidate.stopKind % 10 == selectedEventSubKind and not na(candidate.ref) and not na(reference) and math.abs(candidate.ref - reference) <= math.max(activeRetestTolerance, ctx.retestToleranceValue)
        bool preserveQualified = sameSetup and candidate.priority >= selectedEventPriority
        if sameSetup and reversalNow
            candidate.reversalEligible := true
        bool existingExecutable = candidate.dir == directionFilter and not na(candidate.eventBar) and bar_index - candidate.eventBar <= storedCandidateMaximumBars and (na(candidate.stop) or (isLong ? close >= candidate.stop - candidateInvalidationBuffer : close <= candidate.stop + candidateInvalidationBuffer)) and (na(candidate.ref) or math.abs(close - candidate.ref) <= ctx.safeAtrValue * 1.50)
        bool preserveStronger = candidate.dir == directionFilter and not sameSetup and candidate.priority >= selectedEventPriority and existingExecutable and not (retestNow and candidate.directBreak)
        if not preserveQualified and not preserveStronger
            bool priorReversal = sameSetup and candidate.reversalEligible
            candidate.dir := directionFilter
            candidate.eventKind := selectedEventKind
            candidate.priority := selectedEventPriority
            candidate.momentumExtensionUsed := false
            candidate.eventBar := bar_index
            candidate.ref := reference
            candidate.rng := measuredRange
            candidate.waveOrigin := earlyNow ? na : abcWaveOrigin
            candidate.nRange := earlyNow ? na : abcWaveRange
            candidate.stop := stopAnchor
            candidate.stopKind := packedStopKind
            candidate.usesGuard := horizontalBreak or trendBreak or structureBreak or retestNow and not na(reference)
            candidate.guardLevel := horizontalBreak ? horizontalLevel : trendBreak ? trendBreakReference : structureBreak ? swingReference : retestNow ? reference : na
            candidate.sourceKind := sourceKind
            candidate.directBreak := directNow
            candidate.reversalEligible := priorReversal or reversalNow
            candidate.tolerance := ctx.retestToleranceValue
            activeRetestTolerance := ctx.retestToleranceValue
    int activeMaximumBars = candidate.directBreak ? math.max(ctx.retestWindowValue, maximumBars) : maximumBars
    bool withinWindow = candidate.dir != 0 and not na(candidate.eventBar) and bar_index - candidate.eventBar <= activeMaximumBars
    bool stopHolds = candidate.dir == 1 ? na(candidate.stop) or close >= candidate.stop - candidateInvalidationBuffer : candidate.dir == -1 ? na(candidate.stop) or close <= candidate.stop + candidateInvalidationBuffer : false
    bool breakHolds = not candidate.directBreak or na(candidate.ref) or (candidate.dir == 1 ? close >= candidate.ref - activeRetestTolerance * 1.5 : close <= candidate.ref + activeRetestTolerance * 1.5)
    bool active = withinWindow and stopHolds and breakHolds
    float entryReferenceRange = math.max(nz(candidate.rng, ctx.safeAtrValue * 2.0), syminfo.mintick * 10.0)
    float directMaximumExtension = ctx.adaptiveEntryQuality ? math.min(ctx.safeAtrValue * 1.25, math.max(ctx.safeAtrValue * 0.60, entryReferenceRange * 0.25)) : math.min(ctx.safeAtrValue * 1.75, math.max(ctx.safeAtrValue * 0.85, entryReferenceRange * 0.30))
    float retestMaximumDistance = ctx.adaptiveEntryQuality ? math.min(ctx.safeAtrValue, math.max(ctx.safeAtrValue * 0.35, entryReferenceRange * 0.18)) : math.min(ctx.safeAtrValue * 1.25, math.max(ctx.safeAtrValue * 0.50, entryReferenceRange * 0.20))
    bool minimumBreakLevelPass = not candidate.directBreak or na(candidate.ref) or (candidate.dir == 1 ? close >= candidate.ref - activeRetestTolerance : close <= candidate.ref + activeRetestTolerance)
    bool exceptionalMomentum = ctx.earlyEngineEnabled and candidate.directBreak and ctx.volumePassValue and ctx.bodyRatioValue >= 0.55 and (candidate.dir == 1 ? ctx.closeLocationValue >= 0.72 : candidate.dir == -1 ? ctx.closeLocationValue <= 0.28 : false)
    bool wasMomentumExtension = candidate.momentumExtensionUsed
    bool regularExtensionPass = not ctx.preventExtended or na(candidate.ref) or (candidate.directBreak ? (candidate.dir == 1 ? close <= candidate.ref + directMaximumExtension : close >= candidate.ref - directMaximumExtension) : math.abs(close - candidate.ref) <= retestMaximumDistance)
    float extensionFactor = candidate.reversalEligible ? 2.25 : 1.60
    bool momentumExtensionPass = not regularExtensionPass and exceptionalMomentum and not na(candidate.ref) and (candidate.dir == 1 ? close <= candidate.ref + directMaximumExtension * extensionFactor : close >= candidate.ref - directMaximumExtension * extensionFactor)
    bool extensionRecoveryPass = wasMomentumExtension and barstate.isconfirmed and regularExtensionPass and minimumBreakLevelPass and (candidate.dir == 1 ? ctx.bullCandleQuality and close > open and ctx.closeLocationValue >= 0.55 and ctx.vsaSignalValue != -1 : candidate.dir == -1 ? ctx.bearCandleQuality and close < open and ctx.closeLocationValue <= 0.45 and ctx.vsaSignalValue != 1 : false)
    candidate.momentumExtensionUsed := candidate.directBreak and (momentumExtensionPass or wasMomentumExtension and not extensionRecoveryPass)
    bool entryLevelPass = minimumBreakLevelPass and (regularExtensionPass or momentumExtensionPass)
    bool actionAllowed = stockChartAllowed and active and entryLevelPass
    bool actionable = actionAllowed
    [active, actionable]
// ICT premium/discount is tied to the ordered, confirmed swing leg. It is a
// location preference for retests only, never an independent breakout trigger.
f_dealing_range_context(bool enabled, bool retestMode, bool swingCompatible, float lastHigh, float lastLow, int lastHighBar, int lastLowBar, float currentClose) =>
    bool ready = enabled and retestMode and swingCompatible and not na(lastHigh) and not na(lastLow) and not na(lastHighBar) and not na(lastLowBar) and lastHigh > lastLow and currentClose >= lastLow and currentClose <= lastHigh
    int signal = not ready ? 0 : lastLowBar < lastHighBar and currentClose <= (lastHigh + lastLow) * 0.50 ? 1 : lastHighBar < lastLowBar and currentClose >= (lastHigh + lastLow) * 0.50 ? -1 : 0
    [ready, signal]

f_fib_retracement_context() =>
    bool ready = useFibRetracement and swingLevelsCompatible and not na(lastSwingHigh) and not na(lastSwingLow) and not na(lastSwingHighBar) and not na(lastSwingLowBar) and lastSwingHigh > lastSwingLow and lastSwingHighBar != lastSwingLowBar
    bool bullLeg = ready and lastSwingLowBar < lastSwingHighBar
    bool bearLeg = ready and lastSwingHighBar < lastSwingLowBar
    float legRange = ready ? lastSwingHigh - lastSwingLow : na
    [bullLeg ? lastSwingHigh - legRange * 0.382 : bearLeg ? lastSwingLow + legRange * 0.382 : na, bullLeg ? lastSwingHigh - legRange * 0.500 : bearLeg ? lastSwingLow + legRange * 0.500 : na, bullLeg ? lastSwingHigh - legRange * 0.618 : bearLeg ? lastSwingLow + legRange * 0.618 : na, bullLeg ? lastSwingHigh - legRange * 0.786 : bearLeg ? lastSwingLow + legRange * 0.786 : na]


[fibRetracement382, fibRetracement500, fibRetracement618, fibRetracement786] = f_fib_retracement_context()

var array<label> rightLineLabels = array.new<label>(8, na)
if barstate.islast
    f_sync_right_line_label(rightLineLabels, 0, resistanceZoneDisplayVisible and not suppressResistanceZoneVisual, (resistanceZoneDisplayLow + resistanceZoneDisplayHigh) * 0.50, resistanceZoneDisplayCaption, resistanceZoneDisplayColor, safeAtr)
    f_sync_right_line_label(rightLineLabels, 1, supportZoneDisplayVisible and not suppressSupportZoneVisual, (supportZoneDisplayLow + supportZoneDisplayHigh) * 0.50, supportZoneDisplayCaption, #F59E0B, safeAtr)
    f_sync_right_line_label(rightLineLabels, 2, stockChartAllowed and showSupplyDemandZones and demandZoneVisualRelevant, (demandZoneDisplayLow + demandZoneDisplayHigh) * 0.50, demandZoneDisplayCaption, demandZoneDisplayColor, safeAtr)
    f_sync_right_line_label(rightLineLabels, 3, stockChartAllowed and showSupplyDemandZones and supplyZoneVisualRelevant, (supplyZoneDisplayLow + supplyZoneDisplayHigh) * 0.50, supplyZoneDisplayCaption, supplyZoneDisplayColor, safeAtr)
    f_sync_right_line_label(rightLineLabels, 4, stockChartAllowed and showHorizontalSr and fallbackResistanceVisualReady, fallbackResistanceLevel, not na(higherTimeframeResistance) and math.abs(fallbackResistanceLevel - higherTimeframeResistance) <= syminfo.mintick ? "مقاومة HTF" : "مقاومة مفردة", color.white, safeAtr)
    f_sync_right_line_label(rightLineLabels, 5, stockChartAllowed and showHorizontalSr and fallbackSupportVisualReady, fallbackSupportLevel, not na(higherTimeframeSupport) and math.abs(fallbackSupportLevel - higherTimeframeSupport) <= syminfo.mintick ? "دعم HTF" : "دعم مفرد", #F59E0B, safeAtr)

float nearestMaSupportForStop = f_nearest_price_level(false, close, safeAtr * 0.10, sma24, sma50, sma200, na, na, na, na, na, na, na)
float nearestMaResistanceForStop = f_nearest_price_level(true, close, safeAtr * 0.10, sma24, sma50, sma200, na, na, na, na, na, na, na)

// V98.11: dynamic MA/Fibonacci breaks no longer create standalone candidates.
// Restore V78 trend-reaction quality as a qualified retest, not another evidence vote.
float trendBounceTolerance = math.max(safeAtr * trendZoneAtrWidth, syminfo.mintick * 4.0)
bool bullishMainTrendBounce = majorTrendReady and majorChannelBias == 1 and f_true_level_bounce(true, majorChannelBaseNow, trendBounceTolerance, safeAtr, closeLocation, bodyRatio)
bool bearishMainTrendBounce = majorTrendReady and majorChannelBias == -1 and f_true_level_bounce(false, majorChannelBaseNow, trendBounceTolerance, safeAtr, closeLocation, bodyRatio)
bool bullishSecondaryTrendBounce = secondaryRisingTrendReady and f_true_level_bounce(true, secondaryRisingTrendNow, trendBounceTolerance, safeAtr, closeLocation, bodyRatio)
bool bearishSecondaryTrendBounce = secondaryFallingTrendReady and f_true_level_bounce(false, secondaryFallingTrendNow, trendBounceTolerance, safeAtr, closeLocation, bodyRatio)
bool bullishTrendBounce = bullishMainTrendBounce or bullishSecondaryTrendBounce
bool bearishTrendBounce = bearishMainTrendBounce or bearishSecondaryTrendBounce
float bullishTrendBounceLevel = bullishMainTrendBounce and bullishSecondaryTrendBounce ? math.max(majorChannelBaseNow, secondaryRisingTrendNow) : bullishMainTrendBounce ? majorChannelBaseNow : bullishSecondaryTrendBounce ? secondaryRisingTrendNow : na
float bearishTrendBounceLevel = bearishMainTrendBounce and bearishSecondaryTrendBounce ? math.min(majorChannelBaseNow, secondaryFallingTrendNow) : bearishMainTrendBounce ? majorChannelBaseNow : bearishSecondaryTrendBounce ? secondaryFallingTrendNow : na
bool bullishTechnicalBreak = bullishStructuralPatternBreak
bool bearishTechnicalBreak = bearishStructuralPatternBreak
bool bullishTechnicalBounce = bullishStructuralPatternRetest or bullishFvgRetest or bullishTrendBounce
bool bearishTechnicalBounce = bearishStructuralPatternRetest or bearishFvgRetest or bearishTrendBounce
